## 29. sb_rowF_B_ddcedef78352bbfcf20002e9

Question: Count the bath robe(s) and ceiling light(s) and ping pong paddle(s) in this room together. How many are there in total?

```text
<think>
Step 1: I count 1 distinct bath robe instance, each by the frame where it first appears: 1 of 1, frame 10 (right).
Step 2: I count 1 distinct ceiling light instance, each by the frame where it first appears: 1 of 1, frame 21 (middle).
Step 3: I count 2 distinct ping pong paddle instances, each by the frame where it first appears: 1 of 2, frame 25 (right); 2 of 2, frame 26 (left).
Step 4: add them: 1 + 1 + 2 = 4.
So there are 4 instances in total.
</think>

4
```
